Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The magnet variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 682 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 295 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by magnet generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 295. QED.
